### [1] [Translational tiles without spectra in finite abelian p-groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00087)
*Shilei Fan,Mamateli Kadir*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文在三个有限阿贝尔p群中构造了显式的平移铺砌但不具有谱的集合（tiles without spectra），分别是Z_4^4×Z_2^2中的64点子集、F_2^13中的512点子集和F_3^9中的2187点子集。这证明了"铺砌蕴含谱"（tile-to-spectral implication）对有限阿贝尔p群不成立，且对p=2和p=3的初等阿贝尔群也不成立。作者提出了两种组织性机制（两层障碍和纤维-团障碍）来构造反例，并提供了用精确整数算术和穷举搜索的简短自包含程序来验证所有有限性断言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Fuglede猜想在有限阿贝尔群中涉及"谱集与铺砌集等价"的问题，此前已知tile-to-spectral方向在一般阿贝尔群中不成立，但有限p群中的情形尚不明确。本文旨在构造反例证明该蕴含在有限阿贝尔p群中失败，特别关注p=2和p=3的初等阿贝尔群情形。

Method: 作者提出了两种构造机制：(1) 两层障碍（two-layer obstruction）将一个具有两个合适铺砌补集的非谱铺砌集转化为没有谱的铺砌集；(2) 纤维-团障碍（fiber-clique obstruction）将一组具有受控公共Fourier零点的铺砌补集转化为初等阿贝尔群中的反例。所有构造均配有坐标数据，并通过三个简短的、使用精确整数算术和穷举搜索的自包含程序进行机器验证。

Result: 成功构造了三个显式反例：Z_4^4×Z_2^2中的64点铺砌非谱集（p=2的混合群情形）、F_2^13中的512点铺砌非谱集（p=2初等阿贝尔情形）、F_3^9中的2187点铺砌非谱集（p=3初等阿贝尔情形），从而彻底证明tile-to-spectral蕴含在有限阿贝尔p群中失败。

Conclusion: 本文通过显式构造和计算机辅助验证，完全否定了有限阿贝尔p群中的tile-to-spectral方向，表明该蕴含在初等阿贝尔2-群和3-群中均已失效。这为Fuglede猜想在有限群中的研究画上了一个关键句点，并为后续在更一般群类中对谱集与铺砌集结构的研究提供了新的构造工具。

Abstract: We construct explicit translational tiles without spectra in three finite abelian $p$-groups. The first is a $64$-point subset of $\Z_4^4\times\Z_2^2$. The other two are a $512$-point subset of $\F_2^{13}$ and a $2187$-point subset of $\F_3^9$. Consequently, the tile-to-spectral implication fails for finite abelian $p$-groups, and it already fails within the class of elementary abelian groups for both $p=2$ and $p=3$.
  Two elementary mechanisms organize the examples. A two-layer obstruction turns a spectral non-tile with two suitable tiling complements into a tile without a spectrum. A fiber--clique obstruction converts a family of tiling complements with controlled common Fourier zeros into an elementary abelian counterexample. All coordinate data are included. The finite claims are certified by three short, self-contained programs using exact integer arithmetic and exhaustive searches; the accompanying source files recompute every assertion used in the proofs.

</details>


### [2] [Critical points of point charge potentials along lines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00336)
*Goncalo Oliveira*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文证明了Gabrielov-Novikov-Shapiro猜想1.9：由n个点电荷产生的势能函数在任意直线上（非恒定）的限制最多有2n-1个临界点，且该界是紧的（即使对所有正电荷也如此）。证明方法基于与共线电荷产生的辅助平面势能的二元性，并运用Morse理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Gabrielov-Novikov-Shapiro提出的猜想，即由n个点电荷产生的势能函数在直线上临界点数量的上界。该问题涉及势能理论中临界点结构的深层性质，对理解点电荷系统的几何与拓扑结构具有基础性意义。

Method: 利用与由共线电荷产生的辅助平面势能之间的二元性（duality），将该问题转化为辅助势能的分析，再借助Morse理论来证明临界点数量的上界。通过构造达到该界的例子证明界的紧性，包括正电荷的情形。

Result: 成功证明了Gabrielov-Novikov-Shapiro猜想1.9：对任意p>0，由n个点电荷产生的非恒定势能在任意直线上最多有2n-1个临界点，且该上界是紧的（sharp），即使所有电荷均为正也如此。

Conclusion: 该猜想被完全证明，上界2n-1是最优的。所采用的二元性加Morse理论的方法为类似问题提供了新的有效工具。

Abstract: We prove Conjecture 1.9 of Gabrielov-Novikov-Shapiro. More precisely, for every $p>0$, every nonconstant restriction to a line of a potential generated by $n$ point charges has at most $2n-1$ critical points. The bound is sharp, even for positive charges. The proof uses a duality with an auxiliary planar potential generated by collinear charges and Morse theory.

</details>


### [3] [The frameset of the second-order B-Spline along $ab=1/q$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00481)
*Alexander Stangl,Christina Frederick*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文完整刻画了二阶B样条Q2的Zak变换零点集，并由此给出Lemvig-Nielsen关于Q2的Gabor框架障碍的新证明，同时证明在双曲线ab=1/q上这些障碍是紧的（sharp），首次完整刻画出该曲线族上Q2的框架集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lemvig和Nielsen此前给出了Q2的Gabor框架性质的障碍性条件（obstructions），但未解决其框架集的完整刻画。尤其是沿双曲线ab=1/q这一曲线族，框架集的完整特征化问题此前悬而未决。本研究旨在通过Zak变换零点集的完整刻画来推进这一问题的解决。

Method: 本文通过对二阶B样条Q2的Zak变换零点集进行完全刻画，以此为工具重新证明Lemvig-Nielsen障碍定理，并结合对双曲线ab=1/q上特殊结构的分析，验证这些障碍条件的紧性（sharpness），从而获得该曲线上框架集的完整表征。

Result: 得到了Q2的Zak变换零点集的完整刻画；给出了Lemvig-Nielsen障碍的新证明；证明了在双曲线ab=1/q上，这些障碍条件是紧的，即所有未被障碍排除的点都属于框架集，从而首次完整刻画了该族中一条整条曲线上的F(Q2)。

Conclusion: 本文解决了Lemvig和Nielsen提出的开放问题，首次沿着双曲线ab=1/q完整刻画了Q2的框架集，为Gabor框架理论中B样条框架集的完全表征迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: We give a complete characterization of the zero set of the Zak transform of the second-order B-spline $Q_2$. As a consequence, we obtain a new proof of the Lemvig--Nielsen obstructions to the Gabor frame property of $Q_2$, and, along the hyperbola $ab=1/q$, we show these obstructions are sharp: every point they do not exclude belongs to the frame set. This gives the first complete characterization of $\mathcal F(Q_2)$ along an entire curve in this family, resolving a question left open by Lemvig and Nielsen in \cite{Lemniel}.

</details>


### [4] [Asymptotic safety regions for Gabor frames generated by Hermite functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01296)
*Markus Faulhuber,Irina Shafkulovska,Ilya Zlotnikov*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文为Hermite函数h_n的Gabor系框架集建立了新的区域。经典结果（Gröchenig-Lyubarskii）表明当格密度超过n+1时Gabor系构成框架。本文证明：对任意η>0及足够大的n，当ab≤n^{-2/3-η}时该Gabor系构成框架；并在坐标轴附近获得渐近精确结果——当min{a,b}≤n^{-1/2-δ}且ab≤1/2-ρ时构成框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展Gröchenig和Lyubarskii关于Hermite函数Gabor框架的经典结果，在更宽泛的格参数区域（尤其是低密度区域和坐标轴附近）建立新的框架集边界，从而更精细地刻画Hermite函数Gabor框架的充要参数区域。

Method: 利用分析技术对Hermite函数Gabor系统在不同参数区域（ab≤n^{-2/3-η}的一般区域以及坐标轴附近的退化情形）进行框架性质的论证，并结合渐近估计获得精确的阈值。

Result: 证明了两个结果：(1) 对任意η>0及足够大的n，当ab≤n^{-2/3-η}时Gabor系构成L^2(R)的框架；(2) 对任意δ>0、ρ∈(0,1/2)及足够大的n，若min{a,b}≤n^{-1/2-δ}且ab≤1/2-ρ，则构成框架，后者在坐标轴附近是渐近精确的。

Conclusion: 本文显著扩展了Hermite函数Gabor框架的已知区域，特别是在低密度区域和坐标轴附近得到了比经典n+1密度阈值更精确的结果，为理解Hermite函数框架集的结构提供了新的理论边界。

Abstract: The aim of this paper is to establish new regions in the frame sets of Hermite functions $h_n$. A classical result of Gröchenig and Lyubarskii shows that the Gabor system $\mathcal{G}(h_n,a\mathbb{Z}\times b\mathbb{Z})$ forms a frame for $L^2(\mathbb{R})$ whenever the lattice density exceeds $n+1$. We show that, for every $η>0$ and all sufficiently large $n$, the same Gabor system forms a frame whenever $ab\leq n^{-\frac{2}{3}-η}$.
  Moreover, we obtain an asymptotically sharp result near the coordinate axes, i.e., when one of the parameters $a$ or $b$ is small. Namely, for every $δ>0$ and $ρ\in(0,\frac{1}{2})$ and all sufficiently large $n$ we prove that if $\min\{a,b\}\leq n^{-\frac{1}{2}-δ}$ and $ab\leq \frac{1}{2}-ρ$ then $\mathcal{G}(h_n,a\mathbb{Z}\times b\mathbb{Z})$ forms a frame.

</details>

### [5] [The First Two Exceptional Boundaries for Sets with Few Subset Sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00044)
*Lizhong Chen*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究正整数集合A的子集和大小|FS(A)|的分类问题，在Carpenter-Defant-Kravitz已有的分类基础上（覆盖M≤n-4的情形），确定了M=n-3和M=n-2两种新情况下的所有满足|FS(A)|≤T_n+1+M的集合A的完整分类，包括所有等号情形的刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子集和是加性组合学中的经典研究对象。已有文献对|FS(A)|≤T_n+1+M（M≤n-4）的情况给出了完整分类，但更进一步的极端情形（M=n-3和M=n-2）尚未被确定。本文旨在填补这一空白，完成对这两类临界情况的完整刻画。

Method: 本文采用组合与数论相结合的方法。具体地，通过记录两个端点处缺失的子集和，推导出一个转移公式（transition formula），该公式能够将例外配置从较小的n推广到任意n。分类结果涉及正整数集合的原始（primitive）膨胀（dilates），并使用整数总和上界来刻画这些集合。

Result: 在M=n-3情形下，所有满足条件的集合恰好为：正整数原始集B的正膨胀（满足∑B≤T_n+n-3），以及{1,3,4,…,n+1}的膨胀。在M=n-2情形下，整数总和上界增加1，对应集合包括上述两类，以及额外两个例外模板：{2,3,…,n+1}和{1,3,4,…,n,n+2}的膨胀。此外，还确定了所有等号情形，并证明了n=5是临界阈值，同时给出了M=n-2情形下可公度性（commensurability）的刻画。

Conclusion: 本文完成了正整数集被子集和大小约束下的两个相邻极端情形的分类，完整刻画了所有例外配置及其膨胀，并通过转移公式建立了与任意n的统一联系，进一步证明了n=5为该分类成立的最小维度。

Abstract: Let $A$ be an $n$-element set of positive real numbers, and write $\operatorname{FS}(A)$ for its subset sums, including the empty sum. Write $T_n=\binom{n+1}{2}$. For $0\le M\le n-4$, Carpenter, Defant and Kravitz classify all $A$ satisfying $\lvert\operatorname{FS}(A)\rvert\le T_n+1+M$. We determine the next two cases for every $n\ge5$. At $M=n-3$, the sets are precisely the positive dilates of primitive positive-integer sets $B$ with $\sum B\le T_n+n-3$, together with the dilates of $\{1,3,4,\ldots,n+1\}$. At $M=n-2$, the integer total bound increases by one, and the exceptional templates are $\{1,3,4,\ldots,n+1\}$, $\{2,3,\ldots,n+1\}$, and $\{1,3,4,\ldots,n,n+2\}$. We also determine all equality cases and show that $n=5$ is the sharp threshold, including for commensurability at $M=n-2$. The proof records the missing subset sums at the two endpoints and derives a transition formula that extends the exceptional configurations to arbitrary $n$.

</details>


### [6] [Double basic hypergeometric sums via a regularized Jackson $q$-integral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00074)
*Takumi Maesaka*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用Andrews–Askey积分推导Jackson q-积分的全纯延拓公式，从而将终止型特殊化情形下成立的恒等式延拓到非终止情形，并由此得到若干二重和基本超几何恒等式及其特殊情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多q-超几何恒等式仅在终止型（terminating）参数下成立，而将其推广到非终止（nonterminating）情形往往困难。作者希望借助Andrews–Askey积分提供的全纯延拓工具，建立一种系统方法，使得已有的终止型恒等式能够自然延续到非终止情形。

Method: 作者利用Andrews–Askey积分构造Jackson q-积分的全纯延拓公式，以此将终止型条件下建立的恒等式延拓至非终止情形。将此方法分别应用于Sears–Carlitz变换、Rahman的Askey–Wilson多项式生成函数、q-Chu–Vandermonde求和及Rogers的₆φ₅求和，从而推导出相应的二重和基本超几何积分表示。

Result: 首先得到两个二项式型二重和公式的伴随公式；其中第二个公式将Gasper和Rahman的非终止Sears–Carlitz变换以及Rahman的Askey–Wilson多项式生成函数作为特殊情况包含在内。进一步，对q-Chu–Vandermonde与Rogers ₆φ₅求和应用同一方法，得到两个二重基本超几何和的积分表示；后者的平衡₄φ₃特殊化经过Sears变换等价于Ismail、Rahman和Suslov的二重级数变换。

Conclusion: 本文展示了Andrews–Askey积分作为延拓工具在q-超几何恒等式研究中的有效性，为终止型恒等式向非终止型推广提供了一条系统的路径，并统一了若干已知经典结果。

Abstract: Using the Andrews--Askey integral, we derive a holomorphic extension formula for a Jackson $q$-integral. This formula allows identities established for terminating specializations to be continued to the nonterminating case. Our first application yields two companion binomial-type double-sum formulas. The second formula contains, as special cases, the nonterminating Sears--Carlitz transformation of Gasper and Rahman and Rahman's generating function for the Askey--Wilson polynomials. Applying the same method to the $q$-Chu--Vandermonde and Rogers' ${}_6φ_5$ summations, we obtain two further integral representations for double sums of basic hypergeometric type. A balanced ${}_4φ_3$ specialization of the latter identity is equivalent, via Sears' transformation, to the double-series transformation of Ismail, Rahman, and Suslov.

</details>


### [7] [On the Generating Graph of Finite Abelian Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00102)
*Kavita Samant,A. Satyanarayana Reddy*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了有限（非循环）阿贝尔群的生成图Γ(G)的结构，确定了所有生成对的集合，给出了若干结构刻画（如正则性条件、孤立顶点构成子群的条件），建立了两个非同构有限阿贝尔群G和H满足Γ(G)≅Γ(H)的充要条件，并计算了这些图的邻接矩阵和拉普拉斯矩阵的谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成图是群论与图论交叉领域的重要研究对象，反映了群的生成结构。此前对有限阿贝尔群生成图的系统研究尚不充分，作者旨在填补这一空白，深入理解其图论性质。

Method: 通过系统分析有限阿贝尔群的生成对结构，利用群论与图论的工具，研究生成图的各类性质，包括正则性、孤立顶点子群性质、图同构条件，并借助谱图论方法计算邻接矩阵与拉普拉斯矩阵的特征谱。

Result: 确定了有限非循环阿贝尔群生成图中所有生成对的集合；给出了图正则性的条件、孤立顶点构成子群的刻画，以及两个非同构有限阿贝尔群生成图同构的充要条件；计算了邻接矩阵和拉普拉斯矩阵的谱。

Conclusion: 本文对有限阿贝尔群（非循环情形）的生成图进行了系统而全面的研究，获得了结构刻画、同构判定和谱计算等多方面的结果，为生成图的进一步研究奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: The generating graph $Γ(G)$ of a group $G$ is the graph whose vertex set is $G$, where two distinct vertices are adjacent if and only if they generate $G$. In this paper, we systematically study the structure of generating graphs of finite abelian groups (non-cyclic) and determine the set of all generating pairs. Moreover, we give some structural characterizations, in particular, we determine conditions under which $Γ(G)$ is regular, characterize when the isolated vertices form a subgroup, and establish necessary and sufficient conditions for two non-isomorphic finite abelian groups $G$ and $H$ to satisfy $Γ(G)\cong Γ(H)$. Furthermore, we compute the spectra of the adjacency and Laplacian matrices of these graphs.

</details>


### [8] [Varieties of chain complexes and mixed dimer covers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00202)
*Portia X. Anderson,Esther Banaian,Melanie J. Ferreri,Nicholas Mayers,Shiyun Wang,Alexander N. Wilson*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了等方向A型quiver表示中同时作为链复数的表示（即相邻箭头复合为零的结构），证明这些表示的轨道与2×n网格图的混合二聚体覆盖存在双射，并进一步研究了退化序、枚举计数以及与光束路径的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将quiver表示理论与组合对象（网格图的二聚体覆盖）建立联系，从而为理解链复数结构的轨道分类和退化序提供新的组合视角和计数工具。

Method: 将等方向A型quiver表示中满足复合为零条件的轨道与2×n网格图的混合二聚体覆盖建立双射；利用混合二聚体覆盖上的偏序（分配格）构造退化序的加粗关系；使用Claussen和Ovenhouse的矩阵公式枚举这些轨道，并计算Kostant配分函数在高度受限A型根系上的取值；对于均匀维数向量情形，建立与光束在玻璃板间反射路径的对应关系并给出显式生成函数。

Result: 建立了链复数轨道与混合二聚体覆盖之间的双射；证明了退化序是该分配格偏序的加粗；通过矩阵公式完成了轨道的枚举，并将其联系到Kostant配分函数；在均匀维数情形下给出了与光束路径的对应和显式生成函数。

Conclusion: 本文通过网格图二聚体覆盖这一组合工具，为等方向A型链复数表示的轨道结构提供了完整的分类、排序和计数框架，并揭示了与Kostant配分函数及光束路径的深层联系。

Abstract: A quiver representation consists of a collection of vector spaces along with a set of arrows, which are linear maps between these spaces. In this work, we study quiver representations in equioriented type $A$ which are also chain complexes; that is, in which consecutive arrows compose to zero. We show that orbits of these representations under a change of basis action are in bijection with mixed dimer covers of a $2 \times n$ grid graph. The latter object can be endowed with a partial order which is a distributive lattice, and we show that the degeneration order on the orbits of chain complexes is a coarsening of this partial order. In addition, we use recent matrix formulae of Claussen and Ovenhouse to enumerate these orbits. This also computes the Kostant partition function applied to height-restricted, type $A$ roots. When the dimension vector is uniform, we discuss a correspondence with paths of a beam of light bouncing between glass plates and give an explicit generating function.

Abstract: A graph $G$ is $k$-choosable if it has a proper coloring for every $k$-list assignment. Abstract: Pseudosimilar vertices are vertices in distinct automorphism orbits whose deletions produce isomorphic graphs. A two-clock construction gives a square-root deficit uniformly in the order, while a Chinese-remainder construction with many cyclic clocks yields an infinite family of asymmetric graphs with only a polylogarithmic number of vertices left outside the active chain. Abstract: Kalai's cube--simplex conjecture asserts that for all positive integers $\ell,k$, there is an integer $f(\ell,k)$ such that every polytope of dimension at least $f(\ell,k)$ has either a simplex $\ell$-face or a cube $k$-face; let $f_s(\ell,k)$ denote the threshold restricted to simple polytopes. Finiteness of $f(\ell,k)$ is known only for $\ell,k \leq 2$. In addition, Kalai proved that $f_s(2,k) \leq 2k^2$. Here we prove that $f_s(\ell,k)$ is finite for all $\ell \geq 2$ and $k \geq 3$, the first such result beyond $\ell = 2$, with $f_s(2,k) \leq 2k^2-1$ and $f_s(\ell,k) \leq \tfrac{1}{2}k^2\ell\,2^k$ for $\ell \geq 3$. In the opposite direction, we obtain the lower bounds $f(\ell,k) \geq (5\lfloor \ell/2 \rfloor + (\ell \bmod 2) - 1)(k-1)+1$ and $f_s(\ell,k) \geq \max\{4,\,2(\ell-1)\}(k-1)+1$.
  A companion question asks for the minimum possible size of a 3-face within a higher-dimensional polytope. Meisinger, Kleinschmidt and Kalai proved that every rational $d$-polytope with $d \geq 9$ has a $3$-face with fewer than $78$ vertices or fewer than $78$ facets. Here we improve their bound: every convex polytope of dimension at least $15$ has a $3$-face with at most $13$ facets. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: For integers q at least 3 and r at least 1, consider the directed Cayley graph on the cyclic group of order qr whose allowed steps are the integers from 1 through r. The r translates of H by successive multiples of q then form a Hamilton decomposition of the digraph. A q-layer balanced Hamilton starter is called chain-compatible if one arc can be selected from each Hamilton cycle in this decomposition so that the selected arcs form a simple directed path. In both constructions, the compatible deletion chain is the arithmetic step-2 path beginning at 0 and ending at 2r. For q equal to 4, a chain-compatible starter is obtained explicitly whenever r is congruent to 1 modulo 4 and r is at least 9, while for q equal to 3 one exists for all sufficiently large r congruent to 5 modulo 6. The proofs are constructive: the four-layer case uses an ABAB step word, while in the three-layer case a directed rotational terrace is lifted to a 3-layer balanced directed 1-factor and a fixed four-arc trade joins its two cycles. Abstract: The Modulo Krinkle tilings of Imura (arXiv:2506.07638) form a family of non-periodic, spiral monohedral tilings parametrized by a reduced fraction $m/k$ and an integer $t\ge 2$. Conversely, every prototile admits exactly one fan-twist splice decomposition -- no non-adjacent parameters ever splice -- the cut position being $k_1=m^{-1}\bmod k$, and the recursion descends the Stern-Brocot tree to a single unit edge. Abstract: For a finite graph $G$ on $n$ vertices, let $η(G)$ denote the least order of a finite abelian group $Γ$ for which $G$ is an induced subgraph of some Cayley graph of $Γ$. Babai and Sós (1985) settled the worst-case order of magnitude: it is $Θ(n^2)$. We treat $η$ instead as an invariant of the individual graph, minimised over all finite abelian groups rather than over the cyclic groups alone, which is the restriction implicit in the literature on representation numbers modulo $n$. We prove a local order floor: $η(G)$ is at least the maximum of $n$ and twice the largest independence number of a neighbourhood of $G$. This localises at an arbitrary vertex the correspondence of Babai and Sós between induced stars and sum-free sets; a corollary of the classification of maximum sum-free sets in abelian groups does not lower this floor, but restricts which host orders are admissible and so prunes the search. We determine $η$ exactly for paths, where it equals $n+1$, and for complete bipartite graphs $K_{a,b}$, where it equals $2\max(a,b)$ and meets the floor. A Cartesian product bound gives $η(P_m \,\square\, P_m) = (1+o(1))n$. We report certified exact values of $η$ for $22$ graphs, computed over all abelian groups. Seventeen of the $22$ optimal hosts are cyclic, so on most of these graphs the cyclic restriction costs nothing; where it bites, however, it is expensive. A search restricted to cyclic groups returns $36$ for the Petersen graph against the true value $16$, and $59$ for the Frucht graph against $27$. The cost of the restriction is concentrated rather than diffuse, and we identify the graphs on which it is paid. We also determine $η$ exactly for the double stars $D_{q,q}$ with $2 \le q \le 6$, obtaining $5q$ in each case. Since $η(D_{6,6}) = 30$ exceeds $2n = 28$, no constant below $15/7$ can bound $η(T)/n$ over all trees.

Abstract: The Erdős--Stone Theorem asserts that if a graph has edge density $1-1/r+δ$ then it contains a complete $(r+1)$-partite graph with $b$ vertices in each part, where $b=b_n(r,δ) \gg 1$. The celebrated Chvátal--Szemerédi theorem determined the exact order of $b_n(r,δ)$ for every $δ< 1/r^3$. Their bound, however, is not tight when $δ=1/r-ε$, that is, when the graph has edge density $1-ε$ for small $ε$. Our main result in this paper determines the correct order in this remaining regime, thereby enabling us to give a tight bound for the Erdős--Stone problem for all edge densities. More precisely, we prove that for every integer $r\geq 2$ and $0< δ< 1/r$ we have $$ b_n(r,δ)=Θ\left(\frac{\log n}{(1/r-δ)r\log(1/δ)}\right)\;. $$ The lower bound is obtained using a Kövari-Sós-Turán-type argument combined with a variant of Nikiforov's method of constructing large blow-ups, while the upper bound is proved using a correlated random graph construction, related to tensor powers.

</details>

Abstract: The queen's graph $Q_n$ has the squares of the $n\times n$ chessboard as vertices, with adjacency defined by a common row, column, or diagonal. We give an explicit set of 111 pairwise nonattacking queens on $Q_{221}$. The set contains no queen on an outer row or column and satisfies the original type-A 1-cover conditions of Ostergard and Weakley with parameters $(e,f,u)=(24,23,31)$. A direct enumeration checks all $221^2=48{,}841$ board squares and finds none uncovered. The lower bound of Finozhenok and Weakley therefore gives $γ(Q_{221})=i(Q_{221})=111$. Neuhaus previously established the equality for ordinary domination. The no-edge-square branch of the type-A amplification theorem gives $γ(Q_N)\leq(112/221)N+O(1)$ and $i(Q_N)\leq(113/221)N+O(1)$. These coefficients improve, respectively, the coefficients $30/59$ and $91/177$ stated by Neuhaus. We also describe the exact four-family matching model used to obtain the certificate. An assignment-dual identity gives a lossless reduced-cost deletion rule, and alternating allowed-edge tests give a second lossless reduction. The complete coordinates, two independently implemented standard-library certificate verifiers, and a deterministic reduction audit accompany the manuscript.

Abstract: For $n\in\mathbb{N}$, we consider the set of lattice paths from $(0,0)$ to $(n,n)$ using only unit north and east steps. Given a point $B$ to be avoided, we ask: at which point $A$ on the grid with corners $(0,0)$ and $(n,n)$, different from the endpoints, does the largest number of $B$-avoiding lattice paths pass through? We show that for $n\ge 9$, regardless of the location of $B$, the maximum is attained at one of ten specific points clustered near the two endpoints of the grid. This stability, however, conceals an interesting anomaly. When the obstruction $B$ lies on the antidiagonal $x+y=n$, the points of maximal traffic migrate from the near-corner points $(1,1)$ and $(n-1,n-1)$ to boundary points in the set of possible maximizers. The migration occurs for every $8\le n\le 375$, and intermittently up to $n=495$. We conjecture that the anomaly disappears for $n\ge 496$.

Abstract: We prove a rectifiability theorem for harmonic maps into DM-complexes. Abstract: We study polygonal general helices in Euclidean three-space using the turning and signed torsion angles of the discrete Frenet frame. These characterizations reconstruct the helical axis and the helix angle and yield a sharp bound for each turning angle. We also give a spherical formulation through the tangent indicatrix: its vertices lie on a plane section of the unit sphere, which is a small circle in the non-orthogonal case, whereas the orthogonal case is exactly planar. Abstract: Triangulate a simple quadrilateral by connecting all vertices to an additional point. If the vertices carry values, the piecewise linear function can be assigned a Dirichlet energy. Abstract: Ricci curvature measures the average stability of geodesics under transverse perturbations, but how it should be defined in infinite dimensions is often unclear. Additionally, we explore potential applications for hydrodynamics through the Lyapunov stability of gravest wave modes and Arnold's tradewind estimates for long-term weather predictability. Our framework extends to a wide range of settings. We demonstrate this by introducing Ricci curvature on the state spaces of fluids on rectangular domains, the Lagrangian averaged Euler equation induced by the $H^1$-Sobolev metric, and the quasi-geostrophic equation incorporating the Coriolis effect.

Abstract: We establish codimension-independent mass bounds for geometrically confined area-minimizing rectifiable currents. No classical $C^2$ rotational torus can satisfy the soliton equation, so the loss of regularity is unavoidable within the rotational toroidal class.

Abstract: A long-standing open problem asks whether there exists an infinite-dimensional Banach space on which every bounded linear operator attains its norm. Building on the result for $c_0$, we undertake a systematic study of this equality within a natural family of $\ell_1$-preduals given by hyperplanes of $c$, obtaining a complete characterization in this setting. In particular, we prove that \[ \mathrm{NA}_2(c,c)\neq \mathcal{L}(c,c), \qquad\text{whereas}\qquad \mathrm{NA}_3(c,c)=\mathcal{L}(c,c). \] We also establish Holub--Mujica-type theorems for the classes $\mathrm{NA}_1$ and $\mathrm{NA}_2$. More precisely, under suitable separability and approximation property assumptions, the identity $\mathcal L(X,Y)=\mathrm{NA}_1(X,Y)$ forces every operator from $X$ into $Y$ to be compact, whereas $\mathcal L(X,Y)=\mathrm{NA}_2(X,Y)$ forces every weakly compact operator from $X$ into $Y$ to be compact. Finally, strengthening a construction of Ostrovskii, we show that every infinite-dimensional Banach space admits an equivalent norm and a projection whose second adjoint does not attain its norm.

Method: 方法框架：（1）使用硬dyadic Taylor投影Δ_j构造符号的dyadic分解X_j=2^{-j/p'}Δ_j g'，将算子问题转化为向量值嵌入问题H^t→ℓ^t(H^2)；（2）通过该嵌入范数的平方，将其归结为正列算子b↦∑_j b_j|X_j|^2从ℓ^{p/2}到L^{p/2}的范数估计；（3）利用坐标尾部分析给出本质范数估计和紧致性判据；（4）利用Hardy对偶得到等价的para乘积表述；（5）研究判据在合法解析dyadic分解下的定量不变性，建立Calderón符号空间与Hilbert值乘子空间的等价关系；（6）采用Poisson混合原子测试建立精确的测试定理并进行复杂度分析。

Result: 主要结果：（1）H_g:H^p→H^p有界当且仅当嵌入h↦(X_jh)从H^t到ℓ^t(H^2)有界（2<p<∞, t=2p/(p-2)），该嵌入范数的平方等于正列算子从ℓ^{p/2}到L^{p/2}的范数；（2）坐标尾部给出了本质范数的估计和精确的紧致性判据；（3）Hardy对偶导出等价的para乘积表述；（4）判据在合法解析dyadic分解下定量不变，定义了与Hilbert-range乘子空间相等的、与分解无关的Calderón符号空间；（5）构造了有界但非紧的密频（dense frequency）符号，它不属于已知的块状充分类；（6）证明精确Poisson原子测试定理：有限正Poisson混合可恢复全范数，但不存在固定原子数足以恢复范数；（7）通过聚和概率密度得到内禀的原子复杂度公式和有限带宽测试界。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一般解析符号g所定义的广义Hilbert变换在Hardy空间上有界性的完全刻画，揭示了其与向量值嵌入范数、正列算子范数之间的深层等价关系，并确认该刻画在合法dyadic分解下具有定量（resolution-independent）不变性。重要的创新是这类算子的紧致性本质在于dyadic坐标尾部的衰减，而非仅靠符号的经典充分条件所能捕捉。Poisson原子测试定理及复杂度公式为实际数值计算提供了理论基础，而构造的反例则表明现有块状充分类并不能穷尽有界符号。这些结果统一并推广了此前关于此类算子的诸多零散结论。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal H_g f(z)=\int_0^1 f(t)g'(tz),dt$, and let $2<p<\infty$. Set $t=2p/(p-2)$ and $X_j=2^{-j/p'}Δ_j g'$, where $Δ_j$ is the hard dyadic Taylor projection. We prove that $\mathcal H_g:H^p\to H^p$ is bounded if and only if $h\mapsto(X_jh)_{j\geq0}$ is bounded from $H^t$ to $\ell^t(H^2)$. The square of this embedding norm equals the norm of the positive column operator $b\mapsto\sum_j b_j|X_j|^2$ from $\ell^{p/2}$ to $L^{p/2}$. Coordinate tails yield essential-norm estimates and an exact compactness criterion, while Hardy duality gives an equivalent paraproduct formulation. The criterion is quantitatively invariant under admissible analytic dyadic resolutions and defines a resolution-independent Calderón symbol space equal to the Hilbert-range multiplier space. We construct a bounded noncompact dense-frequency symbol outside the known blockwise sufficient class. We also prove an exact Poisson-atomic testing theorem: finite positive Poisson mixtures recover the full norm, but no fixed atom count suffices. Finally, aggregate probability densities give an intrinsic atomic-complexity formula and a finite-bandwidth testing bound.

</details>

Abstract: We prove a uniform spectral-gap estimate at every even exponent for isometric representations on arbitrary closed subspaces of real $L_p$-spaces. As an application, this result, combined with the work of Bader, Furman, Gelander and Monod, gives an affirmative answer to their Question 4.1: Kazhdan's property $(T)$ implies property $(T_X)$ whenever $X$ is a closed subspace or a quotient of a real $L_p$-space, for every $1<p<\infty$.

### [67] [On cost-induced Santaló-type inequalities in Polish measure spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01460)
*Dylan Langharst,Andreas Malliaris,Michael Roysdon*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，用于建立m元组Polish测度空间上的Blaschke-Santaló型不等式，通过一个传递原理将涉及代价极集的几何加权不等式提升为Santaló型函数积分不等式（称为代价Santaló不等式）。该框架扩展了文献中已有概念，并应用于推导多个新结果，包括矩阵空间、函数正弦Santaló不等式、Gauss空间和球面空间等。此外还得到了RCD(K,∞)空间的结果、Hamming立方体的离散不等式，以及通过该框架建立任意Polish空间上的广义传输-熵不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Blaschke-Santaló不等式是凸几何中的核心结果，已有多种泛函推广，但缺乏一个统一且可广泛适用的框架来覆盖不同测度空间（包括连续和离散、欧氏和非欧氏空间）上的Santaló型不等式。作者希望建立一个系统性的传递原理，将几何层面的加权不等式与泛函层面的积分不等式联系起来，从而在不同类型的Polish测度空间中推导新的Santaló型不等式，并探索其在最优传输中的应用。

Method: 核心方法是一个传递原理（transference principle）：它提供了一种机制，将涉及代价极集（cost-polar sets）的几何加权不等式提升为Santaló型函数积分不等式，后者被称为代价Santaló不等式。该框架定义在m个由连续代价函数耦合的Polish测度空间上。作者将该原理应用于多个具体场景，包括矩形矩阵空间、Gauss空间、球面空间、RCD(K,∞)空间、Hamming立方体等，以推导具体的新不等式。此外，利用该泛函框架进一步建立广义传输-熵不等式。

Result: 得到了多个新的泛函Santaló不等式：在矩形矩阵空间上的版本、函数正弦Santaló不等式。发现了具有log-concave等周函数的概率空间（如Gauss空间和球面空间）适用于该框架，从而得到这些背景下新的泛函Santaló不等式，特别包括RCD(K,∞)空间的结果。在离散情形下，得到了Hamming立方体的不等式。最后，在最优传输方面，获得了任意满足代价Santaló不等式的Polish空间上的广义传输-熵不等式，并在矩阵空间上给出了具体实例。

Conclusion: 本文建立的代价Santaló不等式框架具有广泛的适用性和统一性，不仅统一并扩展了已有的Santaló型泛函不等式概念，还成功推广到多种新型空间（包括矩阵空间、Gauss/球面空间、RCD空间、Hamming立方体等），并将Santaló型不等式与最优传输理论联系起来，建立了新的传输-熵不等式，展示了该框架在凸几何、泛函分析和最优传输等多个领域的交叉应用潜力。

Abstract: We introduce a framework for establishing Blaschke-Santaló-type inequalities on $m$-tuples of Polish measure spaces coupled together by a continuous cost function. Central to our approach is a transference principle, which provides a mechanism to lift geometric weighted inequalities involving cost-polar sets into functional integral inequalities of Santaló-type. We call these equivalent inequalities cost-Santaló inequalities. This definition expands and includes previous notions in the literature.
  We apply this principle to deduce several new versions of functional Santaló inequalities, including on the space of rectangular matrices and a functional sine Santaló inequality. A surprising development is that probability spaces with log-concave isoperimetric functions fit into our framework, for example, Gauss space and spherical space, leading to new functional Santaló inequalities in these settings. In particular, we obtain results for $\operatorname{RCD}(K,\infty)$ spaces. As a discrete application, we obtain an inequality for the Hamming cube. Finally, we explore applications to optimal transport, utilizing our functional framework to establish generalized transport-entropy inequalities on arbitrary Polish spaces satisfying a cost-Santaló inequality, which we explicitly instantiate for matrix spaces.

</details>

Abstract: Krauel, Shafiq and Wood realized torus \(1\)-point functions for the simple affine \voa{}s \(L(k,0)\) built from \(\alg{sl}(2)\) as vector-valued modular forms attached to a cyclic \(R\)-module structure and to the modular tensor category \(\rep{L(k,0)}\). We extend this to torus \(n\)-point functions, defined as traces of chains of \(n\) intertwining operators winding once around the \(τ\)-cycle. Every coefficient of their local (Laurent, or Puiseux) expansion about a diagonal stratum is again a \vvmf{}, lying, whenever a mild lowest-weight hypothesis holds, in the very \(R\)-modules of \cite{KSW}. Granted a torus-primarity hypothesis verified explicitly below for \(\alg{sl}(2)\), the operator product expansion then reduces leading short-distance behaviour to the classified \(1\)-point theory. For \(\alg{sl}(2)\) we classify the fusion-chain conformal block spaces, identify torus \(n\)-point primary vectors, and treat \(n=2\) in detail, obtaining an explicit \((k+1)\)-dimensional family of vector-valued Jacobi-type forms generalizing the level-\(k\) forms \(η^{3k/2}\) of \cite{KSW}. We also extend \KSW's{} categorical picture, representations of \(\Bthree=\PMod(\surf{1}{1})\) built from a modular tensor category, to representations, on fusion-chain block spaces, of a mapping-class subgroup of the \(n\)-punctured torus generated by \(S\), \(T\), and adjacent braidings, with a Verlinde-type dimension formula and categorical \(S\)-, \(T\)-operators. The explicit \(\alg{sl}(2)\) matrix form of \(S\) beyond this abstract construction remains open.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者通过轴对称方法构造解，利用悬链面（catenoidal）作为连接两个球体的颈部模型，并通过尺度分析确定颈部宽度与体积V的幂次关系（V^(4/3)），从而在体积V充分小时获得光滑、嵌入、紧致的曲面解。

Result: 成功构造了一个轴对称、光滑、嵌入且紧致的非最小化解，该解在V→0时渐近于两个体积各为V/2的球，由一个宽度为O(V^(4/3))的近似悬链面细颈连接。

Conclusion: 该结果表明，在此能量泛函下，除了极小化子外，还存在非最小化的临界域，且其几何形态呈现"双球-细颈"结构，颈部尺度通过V^(4/3)的幂律标度关系精确刻画。

Abstract: We consider the problem of finding critical domains $Ω\subset{\mathbb R}^3$ for the energy functional
  $$
  \mathcal E (Ω) = {\rm Per}\,(Ω) + \frac 12 \iint_{Ω\times Ω} \frac{dx\,dy}{|x-y|}
  $$
  under the volume constraint $|Ω|=V$.
  We look for smooth, embedded, compact surfaces $\partialΩ$ that solve this problem. We construct an axially symmetric, non-minimizing solution that, for a sufficiently small $V>0$, resembles the union of two balls with volume $V/2$ connected by a tiny, approximately catenoidal neck with a width of the order $V^{\frac 43}$.

</details>


### [70] [Exit Times for Brownian Motion and Location Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00209)
*Cole A. Kratz,Jeffrey J. Langford*

TL;DR: 本文研究是否可以从布朗运动首次退出时间的矩（概率数据）中恢复流形上的位置信息。作者证明在多种二维有界或无界域中，退出时间矩可以在对称性意义下确定位置，并引入了"泊松层级"（Poisson hierarchy）作为核心工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wyman和Xi在2023年提出了"能否在流形上听到你的位置"这一几何问题，本文将其推广到概率数据场景：即能否从布朗运动样本路径的退出时间分布中恢复位置信息。这一问题连接了概率论、偏微分方程与几何，具有理论与应用双重意义。

Method: 作者提出"泊松层级"（Poisson hierarchy）作为核心工具，建立布朗运动退出时间矩与一族偏微分方程解之间的联系。基于此，分别对椭圆、抛物线、双曲线形的凸域（边界为圆锥曲线）、无界楔形域、等边三角形域、矩形域以及一般平面域进行分析。

Result: 在椭圆、抛物线和双曲域中，前两个退出时间矩足以在对称性意义下确定位置；在无界楔形域和等边三角形域中也有类似结果；在矩形域中，完整退出时间矩序列可以确定位置；此外还给出了两个一般平面域上的位置检测结果。

Conclusion: 布朗运动退出时间矩确实能够提供足够的几何信息来恢复位置（在对称性意义下），其核心工具"泊松层级"为连接随机过程与偏微分方程提供了一种有效的理论框架，为后续在更一般流形上的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: In 2023, Wyman and Xi asked ``Can you hear your location on a manifold?'' We pose a related question, asking if probabilistic data can be used to recover location within a manifold. More precisely, we ask if location can be recovered from the distribution of exit times of Brownian sample paths. We show that exit time moments determine location up to symmetry for a variety of two-dimensional domains. Beginning with (convex) domains whose boundaries are conic curves, we show that in elliptic, parabolic, and hyperbolic domains, the first two exit time moments are sufficient to detect location up to symmetry. We establish similar results in unbounded wedge domains and equilateral triangular domains. We show that the full sequence of exit time moments determines location in rectangular domains. We end with two location detection results on general planar domains. The key tool driving our work is a ``Poisson hierarchy,'' which establishes a connection between exit time moments of Brownian motion and solutions to a family of PDE problems.

</details>

Abstract: We establish higher-order Gaussian upper bounds for the heat semigroups associated with a broad class of sectorial maximally subelliptic quadratic forms on manifolds with boundary. Abstract: The initial-to-final-state inverse problem for the Schrödinger equation consists in determining uniquely the Hamiltonian that generates the evolution, assuming the knowledge of the initial-to-final-state map. This functional maps each initial state $f\in L^2(\mathbb{R}^n)$ to the corresponding final state at a fixed time $T$. The problem was formulated by Caro and Ruiz in the case of Hamiltonians arising from time-dependent bounded electric potentials that exhibit super-exponential decay at infinity. Caro, Parissis and the authors of this article established that uniqueness also holds for time-independent bounded potentials with super-linear decay at infinity.

In the case of time-dependent potentials we get a stability of logarithmic type. A notable improvement is achieved when the potentials are time-independent, where under this assumption we prove Hölder stability estimates.

Abstract: We study quantitative stability for the fractional Yamabe functional introduced by González and Qing. Its minimizers correspond to conformal metrics with constant fractional curvature. We prove that the deficit of the fractional Yamabe functional controls, with a suitable power, the distance to the set of minimizers. Our approach is based on a Lyapunov--Schmidt reduction for the nonlocal functional, together with the extension characterization of the fractional conformal Laplacian, which provides the regularity theory needed for the local analysis. As a consequence, we also obtain an analogous quantitative stability estimate for the associated extension functional.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Willmore流是曲面几何流中的一个核心对象，其Möbius不变性使其在共形几何中具有特殊地位。长期以来，Willmore流在有限时间内可能发生奇点，而奇点处的几何终点（geometric endpoint）是否唯一是一个悬而未决的关键问题。此前的工作（如Jakob的结果）仅能证明子序列收敛性，无法排除不同子序列收敛到不同极限的可能性。因此，本文旨在消除这种子序列依赖性，建立全轨迹层面的唯一性收敛结果。

Method: 本文方法的核心工具是内在输运估计（intrinsic transport estimate），它提供了固定域上诱导面积测度的定量全变差收敛，以及对pushforward测度的有界Lipschitz Cauchy控制。这一估计在均匀定量非脐条件下成立，从而将之前仅有的子序列紧致性（通过Allard紧致性和可整流性）升级为全轨迹varifold收敛。对于能量界$8\pi$的情况，结合Jakob的子序列备选并使其序列无关化，得到了更精确的极限结构刻画。对于Hopf环面轨迹，利用非脐性的自动成立简化了分析。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 在均匀定量非脐条件下，有限时间Möbius不变Willmore流的终点是唯一的，多重点计数varifold全轨迹收敛；(2) 极限varifold具有$L^2$中的广义欧氏平均曲率，满足端点下半连续性；(3) 在初始能量$\leq 8\pi$时，极限要么为零，要么是单位密度、嵌入Lipschitz且亏格为0或1的曲面；(4) Hopf环面轨迹的锚定剖面在$W^{2,2}$弱收敛、在$W^{1,2}$和$C^{1,\alpha}$（$\alpha<1/2$）强收敛；(5) 无穷时间情形在有限耗散长度条件下同样有唯一极限。

Conclusion: 本文解决了Möbius不变Willmore流奇点终点唯一性的问题，建立了从子序列紧致性到全轨迹收敛的升级路径。内在输运估计被证明是分析这种几何流终点行为的强大工具，其应用不仅限于有限时间，还可推广到无穷时间。结果对理解Willmore流在奇点处的渐近行为以及相关的共形几何问题具有重要意义。

Abstract: We prove uniqueness of finite-time geometric endpoints for the Möbius-invariant Willmore flow in $\mathbb{S}^3$ under uniform quantitative nonumbilicity. The multiplicity-counting varifolds converge, without reparametrization or Möbius renormalization, to a unique integral two-varifold. An intrinsic transport estimate gives quantitative total-variation convergence of the induced area measures on the fixed domain and bounded-Lipschitz Cauchy control of their pushforwards. Together with Allard compactness and rectifiability, this upgrades subsequential compactness to full-trajectory varifold convergence. The limit has generalized Euclidean mean curvature in $L^2$ with the natural endpoint lower-semicontinuity bound. For finite maximal trajectories with initial energy at most $8π$, Jakob's subsequential alternative becomes sequence independent: the limit is zero, or it has unit density and embedded Lipschitz support of genus zero or one. For Hopf-torus trajectories under the same energy bound, nonumbilicity is automatic and the anchored constant-speed profiles converge weakly in $W^{2,2}$ and strongly in $W^{1,2}$ and $C^{1,α}$ for every $α<\frac{1}{2}$. At infinite time, the same method yields a unique limit under an additional finite-dissipation-length condition.

</details>

Abstract: The magnetized Vlasov--Poisson equation is a fundamental kinetic model for collisionless plasmas. We establish its quasi-neutral limit in two dimensions in the presence of a spatially inhomogeneous and time-dependent external magnetic field. In the same setting, we also establish the combined mean-field and quasi-neutral limit of the associated repulsive Coulomb particle system, thereby extending the results of \cite{han2021newton}. Both limits lead to the incompressible Euler equations with Lorentz forcing. A key structural feature is the pointwise skew-symmetry of the Lorentz operator, which produces exact cancellations in the modulated-energy estimates and is crucial for controlling the limiting dynamics. We further derive a closed vorticity formulation in which the sum of the fluid vorticity and the magnetic field is transported by the flow, coupled to an evolution equation for the spatial mean velocity. Finally, we establish global well-posedness of the resulting limiting system in the Lipschitz class.

Abstract: In this paper, we study the evolution of nearly radial concentrated vortices for the incompressible Euler equation in a bounded planar domain. We also establish an analogous result for a pair of opposite-sign vortices near a strict local minimum point of the corresponding Kirchhoff--Routh function. No symmetry is imposed on the domain or the initial data, and the initial data need not be close to any steady state. To prove these results, we develop a new Lyapunov mechanism for the evolution of vorticity governed by the Euler equation starting from such initial data, providing quantitative control of both vortex shape and location. Specifically, we combine kinetic-energy conservation and vorticity equimeasurability with several fixed-time estimates to obtain a conditional estimate, and then use a set-valued first-exit argument to propagate it globally in time.

Abstract: We establish the diffusive limit of the stationary neutron transport equation with velocity-dependent inflow data in polygonal domains. We also give a complete formulation and well-posedness theory for the kinetic wedge layer, including its algebraic decay. The proof combines a characteristic stability estimate, a weighted Mellin mapping theorem, a two-depth construction and matching scheme, and a shifted superharmonic barrier for the wedge corrector.

Method: 论文首先对给定的变分不等式 min{u, (−Δ)^s u − (c − ⟨Ax,x⟩)} = 0 证明其独特衰减粘性解的显式结构。具体地，证明解可表示为 u(x)=K max{1−⟨Bx,x⟩,0}^{1+s} 的形式，其中 K>0，B 为正定对称矩阵。证明过程涉及分数Laplacian的性质、粘性解理论以及适定的线性化问题的Fredholm可解性等工具。随后针对 s=1/2、n≥2 的特殊情形，将所得结果与 Fernández-Real 和 Yu 的已有分类结果相结合，推导出三次全局薄障碍解的几何刻画。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 所研究障碍问题的独特衰减粘性解具有显式的“支撑幂函数”形式，正集恰好为一个椭球；(2) 在 s=1/2 且 n≥2 时，非空有界正集的立方全局薄障碍解的正集是椭球，从而证明了 Fernández-Real 和 Yu 在该情形下的猜想。

Conclusion: 本文通过严格的解析方法给出了分数阶障碍问题的显式解，并验证了立方全局薄障碍解在非空有界正集情形下的椭球性猜想。此结果对分数阶自由边界问题和解的结构理论具有重要意义，为后续研究提供了精确的基准例子和分类工具。

Abstract: Let \(n\ge1\), \(0<s<1\), \(c>0\), and let \(A\) be a positive definite symmetric matrix. We prove that the unique decaying viscosity solution of \[ \min\{u,\,(-Δ)^s u-(c-\langle Ax,x\rangle)\}=0 \qquad\text{in }\mathbb R^n \] has the form \[ u(x)=K\max\{1-\langle Bx,x\rangle,0\}^{1+s} \] for some \(K>0\) and some positive definite symmetric matrix \(B\). In particular, its positivity set is an ellipsoid. For \(s=1/2\) and \(n\ge2\), this result, combined with the classification of Fernández-Real and Yu, implies that cubic global thin obstacle solutions with nonempty bounded positivity set have ellipsoidal positivity sets. This proves the conjecture of Fernández-Real and Yu in the nonempty bounded-positivity case.

</details>

Abstract: We develop a rigorous analytical framework for a class of physiologically structured population models with two internal state variables, nonlocal ecological feedbacks, dynamic resources, inter-zone transfer, and selective harvesting. The full model is a coupled nonlinear PDE--ODE transport--renewal system with endogenous inflow at the recruitment boundary, a setting in which transport, nonlocal dependence, and boundary renewal interact at the same level. For this full nonautonomous multi-zone system, we prove finite-horizon well-posedness in a positive $L^{1}$-based state space, including global existence on arbitrary bounded time intervals, uniqueness, nonnegativity, and continuous dependence on initial data, environmental forcing, and harvesting effort. We then isolate an autonomous single-zone reduction at extinction and construct a positive compact next-generation operator on the recruit space. In a further nonlinear stationary reduction, we prove that supercriticality of the basic reproduction number $\mathcal R_{0}>1$ yields existence of a nontrivial stationary state under a parametrized compact-operator hypothesis encoding density-dependent renewal feedback. Finally, for a finite-horizon harvest objective over a compact Lipschitz-regular admissible class, we establish existence of an optimal control. The results separate what can be proved for the full climate-explicit system from what can be justified only after autonomous reduction, thereby clarifying the mathematical scope of threshold and control theory for structured populations.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 该工作提供了Bethuel平面内部理论的Neumann边界扩展，建立了边界情形下界面测度的几何结构理论，表明即使存在边界浓度，应力-能量张量仍能有效地识别势能测度为平稳界面测度。

Abstract: We study asymptotic properties of critical points of the two-dimensional vectorial Allen-Cahn energy with finitely many non-degenerate wells, subject to a homogeneous Neumann boundary condition. For any sequence with uniformly bounded energy, we prove that the limiting full and potential measures are supported on a closed countably 1-rectifiable set up to the boundary and satisfy the discrepancy relations. The potential measure defines a free-boundary stationary rectifiable varifold. Boundary mass may occur: weights are constant on regular boundary arcs, finite-type junctions obey projected balance, and the sole non-boundary branch meets the boundary orthogonally. This provides a Neumann-boundary extension of Bethuel's planar interior theory. The key idea is to read the boundary geometry from the limiting stress-energy tensor, which identifies the potential-energy measure as the stationary interfacial measure even in the presence of boundary concentration.

</details>

Result: 在势函数 Ω 满足 L^(n,q)（q∈[1,2]）反对称条件下，成功建立了椭圆系统解的连续性，并通过构造两种规范结构（SO(m)-旋转与 GL(m)-Gauge）将问题转化为可处理的守恒律框架。

Abstract: Let $1 \leq q \le 2$ and denote by $2 \leq q'$ its corresponding conjugate exponent. We prove the continuity of solutions $u \in W^{1,(\frac{n}{n-1},q')}(B^n, \mathbb{R}^m)$ to the critical elliptic system $-Δu = Ω\cdot \nabla u$ in dimension $n \ge 3$, where the potential $Ω\in L^{(n,q)}(B^n, \mathfrak{so}(m) \otimes \wedge^1)$ is antisymmetric. First, we construct $P \in W^{1,(n,q)}(B^n, \mathrm{SO}(m))$ such that the PDE can be rewritten as $-\operatorname{div}(P^{-1}du) = \ast dξ\cdot P^{-1}du$, which is nearly a Jacobian structure up to the rotation $P$. Second, we provide a Rivière's $\mathrm{GL}(m)$-Gauge in order to establish a "full" $(A,B)$-conservation law, i.e. $-\operatorname{div}(Adu)=d^\ast B \cdot du.$

Abstract: We establish sharp mixed spectral Barron regularity for eigenfunctions of molecular Coulomb Hamiltonians. The mixed norm is a Fourier $L^1$ norm with one isotropic weight and coordinate-product weights, and therefore detects regularity invisible to the isotropic Barron scale. For a nonempty set $I$ of electron indices on which the wave function is antisymmetric, we derive an explicit admissible region for the isotropic order $s$ and the coordinate orders $α,β$. This region is optimal as a uniform statement over the class of clamped-nuclei Coulomb Hamiltonians. For fixed-spin components with two occupied spin blocks, it reduces to $s+α+β<1$; in the fully spin-polarized class it reduces to $s+α<1$. In particular, if $\mathcal I_σ$ denotes the family of occupied same-spin blocks determined by $σ$, then every fixed-spin spatial component $ψ_σ$ satisfies, for every $0\leqα<1$, \[ \left(\sum_{I\in\mathcal I_σ}\prod_{i\in I}\langleξ_i\rangle^α\right)\widehat{ψ_σ}\in L^1(\mathbb{R}^{3N}). \] For a fully spin-polarized state, $\mathcal I_σ=\{\{1,\ldots,N\}\}$.

</details>

Abstract: We consider the Vlasov-Maxwell system with a repulsive point charge. Under a spherical symmetry condition, this reduces to the relativistic Vlasov-Poisson system with a point charge. Within this framework, we prove global existence of classical solutions for small initial data and establish their modified scattering behavior. The proof makes use of the ideas developed in the non-relativistic case.

### [88] [Ideal MHD below the classical well-posedness threshold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01093)

Conversely, on large scales volume doubling and Gaussian bounds on this averaged heat semigroup norms imply relative Faber-Krahn inequalities for subsets of prescribed relative measure. Our proof is based on a lower bound for Dirichlet eigenvalues in terms of these averages. The Faber-Krahn dimension is scale-dependent, but converges to the doubling dimension for increasing radii. Conclusion: 该结果表明在一般黎曼流形上，散度自由流对强正则高斯场的拟不变性条件极为严格，仅等距变换群的流场可以保持高斯测度的拟不变性。

Abstract: We prove an obstruction to quasi-invariance of Gaussian fields under divergence free transport flows on Riemannian manifolds. For the centered Gaussian field with covariance $(1-Δ_g)^{-α}$, $α>\frac{d}{2}$, quasi-invariance under the transport flow is equivalent to invariance. This happens precisely when the flow acts by isometries, or equivalently when the underlying vector field is Killing. The proof leverages the Feldman--Hájek theorem and utilizes localized pseudo-differential computations. On $\mathbb R^d$ this leaves only rigid motions, while on flat tori this leaves only translations.

</details>

Abstract: We study the singular limit of a family of linear degenerate interface transmission problems arising from a regularization procedure in the newly proposed Two-Parameter Diffuse Domain Method (DDM2p). For $α>0$, the regularized problem admits a strictly convex variational formulation on $H^{1}(Ω)$. In the limit $α\to0$, the problem degenerates to a weakly coupled interface system with a nonstandard energy structure. To characterize the limit, we introduce a closed Hilbert subspace $\mathcal{H}\subset H^{1}(Ω)$, defined through an auxiliary Helmholtz problem on an annular subdomain $Ω_2\subset Ω$, and identify the limiting energy functional $\mathcal{E}_{0}$ on $\mathcal{H}$. We prove that the regularized energies $\mathcal{E}_α$ $Γ$-converge to $\mathcal{E}_{0}$ in the strong $L^{2}(Ω)$ topology, using the standard framework. Consequently, minimizers of $\mathcal{E}_α$ converge to the unique minimizer of $\mathcal{E}_{0}$, which is shown to be equivalent to the solution of the limiting interface problem. We further prove strong convergence $u_α\to u_{0}$ in $H^{1}(Ω)$ and establish an $O(α)$ convergence rate. Numerical experiments in one spatial dimension confirm the predicted first-order convergence rate and suggest that this rate is sharp.

Abstract: We prove global well-posedness for the two-dimensional infinite-depth gravity water wave system coupled to a pair of point vortices of opposite strengths. The vortex dipole is initially placed deep below the free surface and oriented so that its leading motion is away from the interface. We show that the vortices retreat almost linearly, and that the velocity induced on the free boundary is therefore integrable in time. Combining this decay with Wu's cubic formulation, together with a transition-of-derivatives and localization argument, we obtain global bounds for small localized perturbations of the flat surface.

</details>

Abstract: A diblock copolymer is a soft-matter composed of two chemically distinct block of repeating monomers covalently bonded together at an end-to-end junction to form a single polymer chain. In this paper, we establish the existence of infinitely many smooth periodic unbounded domain patterns of Delaunay-type in $\mathbb{R}^3$ that optimize the energy distribution in diblock copolymer melts. We emphasize that pattern domains at the equilibrium correspond to stationary sets of the screened Ohta--Kawasaki free energy functional \begin{align*} \mathcal{P}_γ(Ω) := |\partialΩ| + γ\int_Ω\int_Ω G_κ(|x-y|) \,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}y, \end{align*} where $γ>0$, $κ>0$ and $G_κ(r)=\frac{1}{r} e^{-κr}$ is the repulisive Yukawa potential. Equivalently, these equilibria satisfy the corresponding Euler--Lagrange equation \begin{align*} \mathcal{H}_Ω(x):= H_{\partialΩ}(x) + γ\int_Ω G_κ(|x-y|) \mathrm{d}y = \textrm{Const} \quad \text{on } \partialΩ, \end{align*} where $H_{\partialΩ}$ denotes the mean curvature of the surface $\partialΩ$. By analyzing the linearization of $Ω\mapsto \mathcal{H}_Ω$ around flat cylinders and applying the Crandall--Rabinowitz bifurcation theorem, for any $κ> 0$ and sufficiently small $γ> 0$, we prove the existence of non-trivial, $2π$-periodic Delaunay-type equilibrium cylinder interfaces with shapes close to a Delaunay unduloid surface of constant mean curvature.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, we study the fundamental solution of the mixed local-nonlocal operator $ -Δ+(-Δ)^s,$ $ 0<s<1. $ We derive precise asymptotic expansions of the fundamental solution and its gradient, up to the first nontrivial correction term, both near the origin and at infinity. The expansions describe the transition between the local and nonlocal diffusion scales and include the critical regimes in which logarithmic terms occur. As applications of these asymptotics, we establish a distributional Bôcher-type theorem for nonnegative supersolutions with an isolated singularity. We further obtain quantitative positive and antisymmetric maximum principles on punctured balls.

Result: 对于任意可容许初始测度且T̄/T̄c<1，存在保守解在有限时间内形成正零能原子，无需初始低能浓度假设。该凝聚在后续演化中持续存在，并收敛到平衡凝聚质量，同时完整解强收敛到Bose-Einstein平衡态。

Conclusion: 本文建立了从亚临界松驰估计到有限时间Bose-Einstein凝聚的普适路径，推广了先前需要初始凝聚假设的结果，并提供了证明有限时间凝聚的新的分析框架。该结果适用于低动量散射退化指数0≤η<1的量子Boltzmann方程。

Abstract: We prove that semi-strong convergence to a Bose--Einstein equilibrium below the critical temperature implies finite-time Bose--Einstein condensation (BEC) for conservative isotropic measure solutions of the spatially homogeneous quantum Boltzmann equation with low-momentum scattering degeneracy exponent $0\leqη<1$. For every admissible initial measure with $\overline T/\overline T_c<1$, there exists a conservative solution that has a positive zero-energy atom after a finite time without any assumption of initial concentration near zero energy. We also show that it persists and converges to the equilibrium condensate mass, while the full solution converges strongly. The proof introduces a method to obtain condensation from relaxation estimate to Bose--Einstein equilibrium. The relaxation estimate first gives a fixed amount of mass below a small energy level. We then use collision estimates at smaller and smaller energy scales and add their effects in a weighted sum functional with a fixed upper bound. If no atom forms at zero energy, enough mass remains in the positive-energy shells to force this sum to exceed its upper bound in finite time, which leads to condensation in finite time.

*Liening Qiao,Juntao Sun,Jiahong Wu,Fuyi Xu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维周期环面T^3上全可压缩磁流体动力学（MHD）系统，在仅存在流体粘性耗散（磁场无电阻、流场不导热）的极端情况下，证明了当扰动足够小且背景磁场满足Diophantine条件时，系统存在唯一全局光滑解并具有显式代数衰减率，且初始密度无需小性假设，仅要求远离真空和无穷大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在极端耗散机制（仅粘性）下MHD系统的全局光滑性和解的长期行为，特别是揭示磁场和压力在没有自身耗散的情况下如何通过耦合获得有效耗散的隐藏机制。

Method: 采用多层级能量论证（two-tier energy argument）：通过背景磁场与速度的耦合以及Diophantine条件导出的Poincaré型不等式，为压力和磁场扰动建立有效耗散；对中间阶能量使用加权时间衰减估计，精确补偿最高阶密度范数的线性时间增长；在高等Sobolev空间中建立先验估计。

Result: 证明了系统存在唯一全局光滑解，且具有显式代数衰减率。关键突破是：初始密度无需小性假设，仅需有界且远离真空，可允许任意大变化。同时揭示了隐藏的耗散机制——磁场和压力虽无自身扩散或阻尼，但通过与速度的耦合产生有效耗散。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，即使MHD系统在最恶劣的物理条件下（只有流体粘性），依然可以通过背景磁场诱导的耦合效应实现全局光滑性和解的衰减，为理解MHD系统在弱耗散机制下的稳定性提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We consider the three-dimensional full compressible magnetohydrodynamic(MHD) system on the periodic torus $\mathbb T^3$ in the regime where the only dissipative mechanism acting on the system is the viscosity of the fluid: the magnetic field is non-resistive and the flow is non-heat-conducting. We prove that this system admits a unique global smooth solution, together with explicit algebraic decay rates, provided that the perturbation $(\mathbf u_0,\,P_0-\bar P,\,\mathbf H_0-\mathbf n)$ of the equilibrium state $(\mathbf 0,\bar P,\mathbf n)$ is sufficiently small in a high-order Sobolev space and the background magnetic field $\mathbf n\in\mathbb R^3$ satisfies a Diophantine condition. No smallness whatsoever is imposed on the initial density: it is only required to be bounded away from vacuum and from infinity, and may exhibit arbitrarily large variations. The proof uncovers a hidden dissipation mechanism. Although neither the density, nor the pressure, nor the magnetic field is endowed with any diffusion or damping of its own, the coupling of these quantities with the velocity through the background field $\mathbf n$, combined with a Poincaré-type inequality of Diophantine origin, generates effective dissipation for both the pressure and the magnetic field perturbations. The large variations of the density are handled by a two-tier energy argument, in which weighted time-decay estimates for the intermediate-order energy compensate exactly for the linear-in-time growth of the highest-order norm of the density.

</details>


### [97] [Failure of Interior Hölder and Gradient Estimates for the Subcritical Special Lagrangian Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01531)
*Zhenyu Fan,Caiyan Li,Zhehui Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对n≥3维空间中的特殊拉格朗日方程，构造了一族显式的实解析整体解，证明对任意亚临界相及任意0<α≤1，基于振荡的内部C^α估计均不成立，且该解族不存在共同的内部分数阶模量连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特殊拉格朗日方程是几何分析中的重要完全非线性椭圆方程，其正则性理论（特别是内部C^α估计）存在临界正则性问题。作者旨在探究该方程在亚临界相下是否具备基于振荡的Hölder内部估计，以澄清该方程正则性理论中的边界。

Method: 通过构造一族显式的整体实解析解（对整个R^n空间定义的解族），直接对这一族解的振荡行为进行分析，证明其内部振荡衰减速率达不到任何C^α（0<α≤1）正则性要求，从而否证了基于振荡的内部C^α估计的存在。

Result: 对每个n≥3、每个亚临界相以及每个0<α≤1，该显式构造的解族均使基于振荡的内部C^α估计失效；更进一步，该解族不存在共性内部分数阶模量连续性（即无法统一给出任何共同的内部分数阶模量上界）。

Conclusion: 特殊拉格朗日方程在n≥3维空间中的亚临界相下，基于振荡的内部Hölder估计本质性地失败，且其失效程度超越了简单的α临界值——即使考虑任意更弱的分数阶模量，也无法获得统一的内部连续性估计。

Abstract: For every $n\ge3$, we show, by a family of explicit entire real-analytic solutions, that, for every subcritical phase and every $0<α\le1$, oscillation-based interior $C^α$ estimates fail for the special Lagrangian equation in $\mathbb{R}^n$. In fact, this family has no common interior modulus of continuity.

</details>


### [98] [$L^\infty$ Variational Approximation of the Aubry Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01557)
*Hung V. Tran,Yifeng Yu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究周期Tonelli哈密顿系统的临界子解，证明了当Mather商的一维上同调消失时，Evans变分泛函的极小元子序列的一致极限是严格临界子解，其正则集恰好刻画了投影Aubry集，并与后向弱KAM解的一致点集重合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解弱KAM理论与Aubry-Mather理论之间的联系，特别是如何通过变分方法构造并刻画临界子解，以及如何利用子解严格性来精确描述Aubry集的几何结构，为数值逼近Aubry集提供理论基础。

Method: 利用Evans变分泛函极小化序列，通过零温度极限构造临界子解；结合Mather商的一维上同调消失条件、弱KAM解的匹配性质以及Fathi-Figalli-Rifford定理，证明子解的严格性与Aubry集的精确刻画。

Result: 证明了在Mather商满足H^1消失条件时，极限函数u_∞是临界子解且在Aubry集投影之外严格，并给出了Aubry集的两种等价刻画：正则集上保持临界Hamilton-Jacobi等式以及与前向/后向弱KAM解的一致点集。

Conclusion: 该结果在所有光滑Tonelli哈密顿量上对n≤3成立，并提出了通过u_k与其大时间后向Lax-Oleinik演化的近接触集来数值逼近完整Aubry集的原则，为实际计算提供了新途径。

Abstract: Let $H\in C^\infty(\mathbb R^n\times\mathbb T^n)$ be a periodic Tonelli Hamiltonian with critical value $c$. For each $k\in\mathbb N$, let $u_k$ be the normalized minimizer of the variational functional introduced by Evans[7], \[
  I_k[w]=\int_{\mathbb T^n} e^{kH(Dw,x)}\,dx,
  \qquad \int_{\mathbb T^n}w\,dx=0. \] If $u_\infty$ is a uniform limit of a subsequence of $\{u_k\}$ and the Mather quotient $({A}_M,δ_M)$ satisfies $H^1( A_M,δ_M)=0$, then $u_\infty$ is a critical subsolution that is strict outside ${A}$ and \[
  {A}
  =
  \{x\in\mathbb T^n\,:\,Du_\infty(x)\ \text{exists and }H(Du_\infty(x),x)=c\}=\{x\in\mathbb T^n\,:\,u_\infty(x)=u_{-}(x)\}, \] where ${A}$ is the projected Aubry set and $u_{-}$ is the backward weak KAM solution associated with $u_\infty$. In particular, by the theorem of Fathi--Figalli--Rifford[10], this conclusion holds for all smooth Tonelli Hamiltonians on $\mathbb T^n$ when $n\leq3$. This characterization also suggests a natural numerical localization principle for approximating the entire Aubry set through near-contact sets between $u_k$ and its large-time backward Lax--Oleinik evolution.

</details>


### [99] [Uniform stability of expanding simple waves for Euler--Poisson--Boltzmann: a singular compensation approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01589)
*Louis Shuo Wang,Jiguang Yu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文严格证明了暖离子欧拉-泊松系统在柱面R×T上的准中性极限，针对连接不同中性远场的全局光滑扩展平面简单波参考动力学，构建了任意阶德拜展开并建立了与德拜长度无关的一致非线性稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准中性极限是等离子体物理中的核心问题，但在暖离子欧拉-泊松系统中，由于动量方程中存在ε^{-1}惩罚项，证明系统在德拜长度趋近于零时收敛到有效欧拉系统面临本质困难。本文旨在严格建立这一极限并同时保证非线性稳定性。

Method: 核心方法是奇异性补偿机制：通过分部积分流体输运项，并将暖离子对称化器直接与时间微分的非线性泊松约束耦合，提取出ε-一致相对能量拓扑。该拓扑将势约束在H^s范数中、梯度约束在εH^s缩放范数中，从而避开动量方程中致命的ε^{-1}惩罚。

Result: 对每个有限阶M，构造了余项为O(ε^{2M+2})的偶数德拜展开，并在任意固定时间区间[t0,T]上建立一致非线性稳定性，寿命和能量估计完全独立于德拜长度0<ε≤ε0。框架还容纳了真正的二维横向扰动，包括特定涡度的输运，给出了良备数据的定量任意阶准中性展开。

Conclusion: 该工作严格隔离了光滑扩展波的稳定性与中心黎曼扇的几何奇异性，完成了暖离子欧拉-泊松系统准中性极限的全面严格化论证。

Abstract: We rigorously justify the quasineutral limit for the warm-ion Euler--Poisson system with Maxwell--Boltzmann electrons on the cylinder $\R\times\T$. The reference dynamics are governed by a globally smooth expanding planar simple wave of the effective Euler system, connecting distinct neutral far-field states. For every finite order $M$, we construct an even Debye expansion with residual $O(\eps^{2M+2})$ and establish uniform nonlinear stability on any fixed time interval $[t_0,T]$, with a lifespan and energy estimates entirely independent of the Debye length $0<\eps\le\eps_0$. The central analytical contribution is a singular compensation mechanism: by integrating the fluid transport by parts and coupling the warm-ion symmetrizer directly to the time-differentiated nonlinear Poisson constraint, we extract an $\eps$-uniform relative energy topology. This bounds the potential in $H^s$ and its gradient in the scaled norm $\eps H^s$, bypassing the fatal $\eps^{-1}$ penalty in the momentum equation. Consequently, the framework accommodates genuinely two-dimensional transverse perturbations, including the transport of specific vorticity. This yields a quantitative, arbitrary-order quasineutral expansion for well-prepared data, strictly isolating the stability of the smooth expansion wave from the geometric singularities of the centered Riemann fan.

</details>

### [100] [Dominance and equivalence for states on $C^*$-algebras: Quasi-Invariant states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00409)
*Ameur Dhahri,Francesco Fidaleo,Chul Ki Ko,Hyun Jae Yoo*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文研究了$C^*$-代数上态的非交换测度论推广，即态的（次）支配与等价关系，以探索群作用下的拟不变性。文章导出了无界"Radon-Nikodym"导数（属于支配态GNS表示的可换代数），证明支配关系一般非传递；对$*$-自同构群作用，拟不变态轨道内状态互相等价，但轨道闭包可能包含奇异态，说明拟不变态集合对凸组合封闭但非拓扑闭。文中还给出了群作用在GNS Hilbert空间上的酉实现（推广协变表示），并与Pedersen-Takesaki构造（导数位于中心化子而非可换代数中）进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典测度论中的支配与等价概念推广到非交换框架（$C^*$-代数上的态），为理解群作用下拟不变态的结构提供新的视角和数学工具，同时揭示与已有构造（Pedersen-Takesaki）之间的区别与联系。

Method: 利用GNS表示与可换代数理论，定义并分析态之间的支配关系；通过无界闭算子（Radon-Nikodym导数）刻画支配态之间的密度关系；研究$*$-自同构群作用下拟不变态的轨道结构、凸组合闭包性质以及Hilbert空间上的酉实现。

Result: 1）对支配态导出了属于支配态GNS表示可换代数的无界Radon-Nikodym导数；2）证明态支配一般非传递（因相应闭算子乘积可能非闭）；3）拟不变态的群作用轨道内状态两两等价，但轨道闭包可含奇异态；4）拟不变态集合对凸组合封闭但非拓扑闭；5）给出群作用在GNS空间上的酉实现（推广协变表示）；6）与Pedersen-Takesaki构造对比，后者导数位于centraliser而非commutant。

Conclusion: 文章建立了非交换框架下态支配与等价理论的完整图景，揭示了拟不变态的轨道与闭包结构的微妙性质，并澄清了与Pedersen-Takesaki构造之间的结构性差异（导数落位不同：commutant对应可换代数，centraliser对应不变部分），为相关领域提供了新的数学分析工具。

Abstract: We study the noncommutative generalization of measure-theoretic dominance and equivalence of states on $C^*$-algebras to explore quasi-invariance under group actions. For a dominated state, we derive an unbounded "Radon-Nikodym" derivative affiliated with the commutant algebra of the dominating state's GNS representation. Interestingly, this dominance is generally non-transitive because the product of the corresponding closed operators can be non-closable. When looking at group actions by $*$-automorphisms, the orbit of a fixed quasi-invariant state consists entirely of mutually equivalent states. However, the orbit closure may contain singular states, meaning the set of quasi-invariant states is closed under convex combinations but not topologically closed. The paper also provides a unitary implementation of the group action on the GNS Hilbert space-generalizing covariant representations and compares this approach with the Pedersen-Takesaki construction, where the Radon-Nikodym derivative sits in the centraliser instead of the commutant.

</details>


### [101] [Rapid decay and functional calculus in ${\rm C}^*$-probability spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00452)
*Felipe Flores*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文证明：若迹C*-概率空间(A,ρ)相对于滤过L具有快速衰减性质，则相应的Sobolev代数H_L^∞(A,ρ)在A的光滑泛函演算下封闭。由此推出范数估计、理想分离性质以及包含映射H_L^∞(A,ρ)⊂A在K-理论上诱导同构等结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sobolev代数是连接分析性质与代数结构的桥梁，研究其在光滑泛函演算下的封闭性有助于深入理解快速衰减C*-概率空间的结构，并为K-理论同构、理想分离等性质提供统一框架。

Method: 基于tracial C*-概率空间上的滤过L与快速衰减性质，构造相关的Sobolev代数H_L^∞(A,ρ)，并通过光滑泛函演算封闭性的论证，推导出一系列推论。

Result: Sobolev代数H_L^∞(A,ρ)在A的光滑泛函演算下封闭；并由此获得范数估计、理想分离性质，以及H_L^∞(A,ρ)↪A在K-理论上诱导同构等结果。

Conclusion: 带快速衰减性质的tracial C*-概率空间的Sobolev代数具有良好的平稳性，其与原始C*-代数的K-理论一致，表明该代数是研究相关算子代数结构的有效工具。

Abstract: Let $(A,ρ)$ be a tracial ${\rm C}^*$-probability space with the rapid decay property relative to a filtration $L$. We show that the associated Sobolev algebra $H_L^{\infty}(A,ρ)$ is closed under the smooth functional calculus of $A$. Some consequences include norm estimates, the ideal separation property, and the fact that the inclusion $H_L^{\infty}(A,ρ)\subset A$ induces an isomorphism in $K$-theory.

</details>


### [102] [Two-basepoint Terwilliger algebras and the quantum symmetry of prime-order circulants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00462)
*Mohammad F. Marashdeh*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 该论文研究素数阶顶点传递图的量子对称性问题，通过构造双基点Terwilliger代数，提出了一种判定量子置换代数是否可交换的新方法。该方法将Banica-Bichon-Chenevier的阈值从指数级改进为二次界，并解决了Chassaniol遗留的两个开放图例，完成类型≤10的完整分类，并将"量子对称当且仅当完全图或空图"的二分法扩展到所有素数阶p≤250。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Banica、Bichon和Chenevier提出的问题——哪些素数阶顶点传递图具有量子对称性——在Paley图的稠密区域中仍然开放，因为相干代数方法在此区域不提供任何信息。需要新的方法来突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 对每个此类图，构造其分圆方案的带两个基点的Terwilliger代数，并研究该代数从基点生成的模。关键观察是：完整性迫使量子置换代数是可交换的，且该模不存在中间状态——它要么恰好包含两个点质量，要么包含全部p个。因此只需在任意深度捕获一个点质量即可判定，而Chassaniol的轨道准则正是深度为一的情形。基于此，用锐化的计数论证将阈值改进，并提供四个加法模p的短证书来解决具体图例。

Result: 将阈值从p>6^{φ(k)}改进为p>(k-1)(k-2)+2的二次界；通过四个证书解决了C31(2,4,8,15)和C41(4,10,16,18)两个此前由Chassaniol遗留的图，完成了类型≤10的完整分类（无需机器辅助）；将"量子对称当且仅当完全或空"的二分法精确计算扩展到所有素数阶p≤250，解决了p≤241的Paley图Pp——这是P17之后的首批结果。

Conclusion: 新的Terwilliger代数方法在素数阶顶点传递图的量子对称性判定上取得了实质性突破。唯一剩余的关键步骤是捕获单个显式向量：两个基点之间的中点2^{-1}。

Abstract: Which vertex-transitive graphs of prime order have quantum symmetry? The question of Banica, Bichon and Chenevier is open in the dense regime of Paley graphs, where coherent-algebra methods give no information. To each such graph we attach a two-basepoint Terwilliger algebra of its cyclotomic scheme and study the module it generates from the basepoints: fullness forces the quantum permutation algebra to be commutative, and the module admits no intermediate state, containing either exactly two point masses or all $p$ of them. One point mass, captured at any depth, therefore suffices, and Chassaniol's orbital criterion is the depth-one case. Three consequences follow. A sharp counting argument replaces the Banica--Bichon--Chenevier threshold $p>6^{\varphi(k)}$ by the quadratic bound $p>(k-1)(k-2)+2$, where $k$ is the type. Four certificates, each a short list of additions modulo $p$, settle $C_{31}(2,4,8,15)$ and $C_{41}(4,10,16,18)$, the two graphs left open by Chassaniol, and complete the classification for type at most $10$ without machine assistance. An exact computation extends the dichotomy ``quantum symmetry if and only if complete or empty'' to all prime orders $p\le250$, settling the Paley graphs $P_{p}$ with $p\le241$, the first beyond $P_{17}$. What remains is the capture of a single explicit vector: the midpoint $2^{-1}$ of the two basepoints.

</details>


### [103] [Operator-valued maximal $f$-divergences for completely positive maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00554)
*Rui Okayasu*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文为冯·诺依曼代数之间的完全正映射引入了算子值最大f-散度，基于算子凸函数f构造，取值于余域代数的扩展下半有界自伴部分。该构造恢复了Hiai的标量最大f-散度，建立了联合弱下半连续性（回答了Hiai的开放问题），并在特殊情形下获得算子值Belavkin–Staszewski相对熵，且证明其与Hollands–Ranallo的BS信道散度在扩展范数意义下一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典散度理论推广到算子值/量子信息论的框架中。Hiai已建立最大f-散度，但其联合弱下半连续性在一般冯·诺依曼代数上尚未解决；同时，已有的Belavkin–Staszewski相对熵和信道散度缺乏统一的算子值框架，需要一个能统一处理这些概念的一般性构造。

Method: 利用算子凸函数f定义CP映射之间的算子值最大f-散度，证明其与公共CP上界无关；通过分析性质证明联合次可加性、单次预复合和正常后复合的单调性；利用正常CP映射的鞅收敛定理和点σ-弱拓扑下的联合下半连续性论证；通过特化f(t)=t log t获得BS相对熵，并在σ-有限余域条件下证明与Hollands–Ranallo的BS信道散度的等价性。有限维情形下给出显式公式，并在有限指标条件期望情形下将BS公式化简为Jones–Kosaki指标的対数。

Result: 建立了算子值最大f-散度的完备理论性质（独立性、联合次可加性、单调性、鞅收敛、联合下半连续性）；恢复了Hiai的最大f-散度并解决了其联合弱下半连续性的开放问题；在f(t)=t log t时获得BS相对熵，并证明BS信道散度等于算子值散度的扩展范数；给出了有限维和有限指标条件期望的显式公式，BS情形下与Jones–Kosaki指标相联系。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个统一且自洽的算子值散度框架，将Hiai最大f-散度、BS相对熵和信道散度纳入同一理论，同时解决了Hiai遗留的开放问题，并通过与Jones–Kosaki指标的联系揭示了与算子代数几何结构的内在关联。

Abstract: We introduce an operator-valued maximal f-divergence for completely positive (CP) maps between von Neumann algebras, associated with an operator convex function f on (0,+infinity). The construction takes values in the extended lower-semibounded self-adjoint part of the codomain von Neumann algebra. We prove independence of the common CP upper bound used in its computation, joint subadditivity, monotonicity under unital precomposition and normal postcomposition, a martingale convergence theorem for normal CP maps, and joint lower semicontinuity in the point-sigma-weak topology.
  For normal positive functionals, our construction recovers Hiai's maximal f-divergence. As a consequence, we establish its joint weak lower semicontinuity for arbitrary von Neumann algebras, answering a question left open by Hiai.
  For eta(t)=t log t, we obtain an operator-valued Belavkin--Staszewski (BS) relative entropy. Moreover, for normal channels with sigma-finite codomain, we prove that the BS channel divergence of Hollands and Ranallo coincides with the extended norm of our operator-valued divergence.
  Finally, we give finite-dimensional examples and obtain an explicit formula for a finite-index conditional expectation. In the BS case, this formula reduces to the logarithm of the Jones--Kosaki index.

</details>


### [104] [Properly proximal countable measured groupoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01253)
*Adriana Fernández Quero,Kai Toyosawa*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文介绍了可数测度群胚的"适当前近性"概念，将其推广自可数群情形，并证明该群胚性质等价于伴随群胚诺伊曼代数的强化相对适当前近性。文中研究了该性质的持久性，证明其在下有限直积、变换群胚构造和测度等价下保持，并识别了包括横测度群胚和自由积群胚在内的广类适当前近性群胚，同时证明内适群胚永不具有适当前近性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将群论中适当前近性的概念扩展到可数测度群胚的框架，以丰富群胚动力系统与算子代数的研究工具，并理解其在更广泛结构（如变换群胚、自由积）中的行为。

Method: 定义了可数测度群胚的适当前近性概念；通过群胚诺伊曼代数的强化相对适当前近性建立等价关系；利用永久性分析、直积构造、变换群胚及测度等价等手段系统研究性质保持；并通过横测度群胚和自由积群胚等具体类别以及内适群胚的反例来刻画性质边界。

Result: 证明了测度群胚的适当前近性与群胚诺伊曼代数强化相对适当前近性的等价性；确立了适当前近性在有限直积、变换群胚和测度等价下的保持性；识别了横测度群胚与自由积群胚为适当前近性的广类例子，并证明内适可数测度群胚不能是适当前近的。

Conclusion: 成功将适当前近性从可数群拓展至可数测度群胚，建立其与算子代数性质的深层联系，并系统刻画了其永久性与边界案例。

Abstract: We introduce the notion of proper proximality for countable measured groupoids, extending the corresponding notion for countable groups. We prove that this groupoid property is equivalent to a strengthened form of relative proper proximality for the associated groupoid von Neumann algebra. We investigate permanence properties and show, in particular, that proper proximality is preserved under finite direct products, formation of transformation groupoids, and measure equivalence. We also identify broad classes of properly proximal groupoids, including transverse measured groupoids and free product groupoids, and prove that inner amenable groupoids are never properly proximal.

</details>
